Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in phases theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 741 and the invariant I(A,B) = 163 mod 2.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. This completes the proof. QED.
